  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c01",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "In the month of Zip, the feast Ihcil Ixchel was celebrated by the physicians and shamans (hechiceros), and divination stones as well as medicine bundles containing little idols of \"the Goddess of medicine whom they called Ixchel\" were brought forward.",
      "locator": "Identification",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada bulan Zip, pesta Ihcil Ixchel dirayakan para tabib dan dukun (hechiceros); batu ramalan dan bungkusan obat berisi berhala-berhala kecil \"Dewi pengobatan yang mereka sebut Ixchel\" dikeluarkan.",
        "en": "In the month of Zip the feast Ihcil Ixchel was celebrated by physicians and shamans (hechiceros), and divination stones and medicine bundles containing little idols of \"the Goddess of medicine whom they called Ixchel\" were brought forward."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c02",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "Ixchel or Ix Chel is the 16th-century name of the aged jaguar goddess of midwifery and medicine in ancient Maya culture. She corresponds to Toci, an Aztec earth goddess inhabiting the sweatbath.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ixchel atau Ix Chel adalah nama abad ke-16 bagi dewi jaguar tua kebidanan dan pengobatan dalam budaya Maya kuno. Ia sepadan dengan Toci, dewi bumi Aztec yang mendiami pemandian uap.",
        "en": "Ixchel or Ix Chel is the 16th-century name of the aged jaguar goddess of midwifery and medicine in ancient Maya culture, and she corresponds to Toci, an Aztec earth goddess inhabiting the sweatbath."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c03",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "In the 1500s, Diego de Landa called Ixchel \"the Goddess of making children\". He also mentioned her as the goddess of medicine",
      "locator": "Identification",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada tahun 1500-an, Diego de Landa menyebut Ixchel \"Dewi pembuat anak\"; ia juga menyebutnya dewi pengobatan.",
        "en": "In the 1500s Diego de Landa called Ixchel \"the Goddess of making children\" and also mentioned her as the goddess of medicine."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c04",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "Ixchel was already known to the Classical Maya. As Taube has demonstrated, she corresponds to Goddess O of the Dresden Codex, an aged woman with jaguar ears.",
      "locator": "Identification",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ixchel sudah dikenal bangsa Maya Klasik. Seperti ditunjukkan Taube, ia sepadan dengan Dewi O dalam Kodeks Dresden, seorang wanita tua bertelinga jaguar.",
        "en": "Ixchel was already known to the Classical Maya; as Taube has demonstrated, she corresponds to Goddess O of the Dresden Codex, an aged woman with jaguar ears."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c05",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "The name Ixchel was in use in 16th-century Yucatán and amongst the Poqom in the Baja Verapaz. Its meaning is not certain. Assuming that the name originated in Yucatán, chel could mean \"rainbow\".",
      "locator": "Meaning of the name",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Ixchel dipakai di Yucatán abad ke-16 dan di kalangan Poqom di Baja Verapaz. Maknanya tidak pasti; bila nama itu berasal dari Yucatán, chel dapat berarti \"pelangi\".",
        "en": "The name Ixchel was in use in 16th-century Yucatán and among the Poqom in the Baja Verapaz; its meaning is not certain, but if it originated in Yucatán, chel could mean \"rainbow\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c06",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "Ix Chel's Classic name glyph remains to be identified. It is quite possible that several names were in use to refer to the goddess, and these need not necessarily have included her late Yucatec and Poqom name. Her codical name is now generally rendered as \"Chak Chel\".",
      "locator": "Meaning of the name",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Glif nama Klasik Ix Chel belum teridentifikasi. Sangat mungkin beberapa nama dipakai untuk sang dewi, dan itu belum tentu mencakup namanya yang muncul belakangan di Yucatec dan Poqom. Nama kodeksnya kini umumnya dibaca \"Chak Chel\".",
        "en": "Ix Chel's Classic name glyph has yet to be identified; several names may have been used for the goddess, not necessarily including her late Yucatec and Poqom name, and her codical name is now generally rendered \"Chak Chel\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c07",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "In the past, it was common to take Ix Chel as the Yucatec name of the moon goddess because of a shared association with human fertility and procreation. The identification is questionable, however, since (1) colonial and ethnographical sources provide no direct evidence to show that Ixchel was a moon goddess",
      "locator": "Confusion with the moon goddess",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dahulu lazim menganggap Ix Chel sebagai nama Yucatec bagi dewi bulan karena kesamaan kaitan dengan kesuburan dan prokreasi manusia. Namun identifikasi itu diragukan, sebab sumber kolonial dan etnografis tidak memberi bukti langsung bahwa Ixchel dewi bulan.",
        "en": "In the past it was common to take Ix Chel as the Yucatec name of the moon goddess because of a shared association with human fertility and procreation, but the identification is questionable because colonial and ethnographic sources give no direct evidence that Ixchel was a moon goddess."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c08",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "An entwined serpent serves as Ixchel's headdress, crossed bones may adorn her skirt, and instead of human hands and feet, she sometimes has claws.",
      "locator": "Ixchel as an earth and a war goddess",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seekor ular yang melilit menjadi hiasan kepala Ixchel, tulang bersilang dapat menghiasi roknya, dan alih-alih tangan dan kaki manusia ia kadang bercakar.",
        "en": "An entwined serpent serves as Ixchel's headdress, crossed bones may adorn her skirt, and instead of human hands and feet she sometimes has claws."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c09",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "Being a jaguar goddess, the Classic Ixchel (or 'Chak Chel') could equally be imagined as a fearsome female warrior equipped with shield and spear",
      "locator": "Ixchel as an earth and a war goddess",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai dewi jaguar, Ixchel masa Klasik (atau \"Chak Chel\") dapat pula dibayangkan sebagai prajurit perempuan yang menakutkan, bersenjata perisai dan tombak.",
        "en": "Being a jaguar goddess, the Classic Ixchel (or \"Chak Chel\") could equally be imagined as a fearsome female warrior equipped with shield and spear."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c10",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "The Madrid Codex (30b) assimilates Goddess O to a rain deity, with rain pouring from her arm-pits and abdomen, while the Dresden Codex includes her in almanacs dedicated to the rain deities (Chaacs) and typically has her invert a water jar.",
      "locator": "Ixchel as a rain goddess",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kodeks Madrid (30b) menyamakan Dewi O dengan dewa hujan, dengan hujan mengalir dari ketiak dan perutnya, sedangkan Kodeks Dresden memasukkannya dalam almanak bagi dewa-dewa hujan (para Chaac) dan lazim menampilkannya menuangkan kendi air.",
        "en": "The Madrid Codex (30b) assimilates Goddess O to a rain deity with rain pouring from her armpits and abdomen, while the Dresden Codex includes her in almanacs dedicated to the rain deities (Chaacs) and typically has her invert a water jar."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c11",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "Ixchel figures in a Verapaz myth related by Las Casas, according to which she, together with her spouse, Itzamna, had thirteen sons, two of whom created heaven and earth and all that belongs to it. No other myth figuring Ixchel has been preserved.",
      "locator": "Mythology",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ixchel muncul dalam mitos Verapaz yang dituturkan Las Casas: bersama suaminya, Itzamna, ia memiliki tiga belas putra, dua di antaranya menciptakan langit, bumi, dan segala isinya. Tidak ada mitos lain tentang Ixchel yang terlestarikan.",
        "en": "Ixchel figures in a Verapaz myth related by Las Casas in which she and her spouse Itzamna had thirteen sons, two of whom created heaven and earth and all that belongs to it; no other myth of Ixchel has been preserved."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c12",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "In the early 16th century, Maya women seeking to ensure a fruitful marriage would travel to the sanctuary of Ix Chel on the island of Cozumel, the most important place of pilgrimage after Chichen Itza, off the east coast of the Yucatán peninsula. There, a priest hidden in a large statue would give oracles.",
      "locator": "Cult of Ixchel",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada awal abad ke-16, perempuan Maya yang ingin pernikahan subur melakukan perjalanan ke tempat suci Ix Chel di Pulau Cozumel, tempat ziarah terpenting setelah Chichen Itza, di lepas pantai timur Semenanjung Yucatán. Di sana seorang imam yang bersembunyi di dalam patung besar memberikan ramalan.",
        "en": "In the early 16th century Maya women seeking a fruitful marriage would travel to the sanctuary of Ix Chel on the island of Cozumel, the most important place of pilgrimage after Chichen Itza, where a priest hidden in a large statue would give oracles."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c13",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s2",
      "quote": "Para sus partos acudian a las hechizeras, las quales les hazian creer de sus mentiras y les ponian debaxo de la cama un idolo de un demonio, llamado Ixchel, que dezian era la diosa de hazer las criaturas.",
      "locator": "Landa, on childbirth",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk persalinan, perempuan mendatangi para dukun (hechizeras) yang menaruh di bawah ranjang sebuah berhala \"setan\" bernama Ixchel, yang menurut mereka adalah dewi pembuat anak.",
        "en": "For childbirth women turned to sorceresses (hechizeras), who placed under the bed an idol of a \"demon\" called Ixchel, who they said was the goddess of making creatures."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c14",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s2",
      "quote": "sacavan los emboltorios de su medicina en que traian muchas niñerias y sendos dollillos de la diosa de la medicina que llamavan Ixchel, y asi a esta fiesta llamavan Ihcil-Ixchel",
      "locator": "Landa, month of Zip",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka mengeluarkan bungkusan obat berisi banyak pernak-pernik dan berhala kecil dewi pengobatan yang mereka sebut Ixchel, sehingga pesta itu dinamai Ihcil-Ixchel.",
        "en": "They brought out their medicine bundles with many trinkets and little idols of the goddess of medicine whom they called Ixchel, hence the feast was called Ihcil-Ixchel."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c15",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s2",
      "quote": "Conjointement avec Zamna on adorait au Yucatan une déesse qui passait pour la mère des dieux; on l'appelait Xchel ou Ixchel.",
      "locator": "Note on Zamna",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bersama Zamna, di Yucatan dipuja seorang dewi yang dianggap ibu para dewa; ia disebut Xchel atau Ixchel.",
        "en": "Together with Zamna a goddess considered the mother of the gods was worshipped in Yucatan; she was called Xchel or Ixchel."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c16",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s2",
      "quote": "Ixchel, mieux Xchel, suivant la grammaire du P. Beltran, est tout simplement le féminin de Chel",
      "locator": "Note 138",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tata bahasa P. Beltran, Ixchel (lebih tepat Xchel) hanyalah bentuk feminin dari Chel.",
        "en": "According to the grammar of P. Beltran, Ixchel (better Xchel) is simply the feminine of Chel."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c17",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s3",
      "quote": "God O, or Ix Chel, is the goddess of rainbows.",
      "locator": "Ix Chel",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dewa O, atau Ix Chel, adalah dewi pelangi (menurut sumber ini).",
        "en": "God O, or Ix Chel, is the goddess of rainbows (according to this source)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c18",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s3",
      "quote": "In her typical form, Ix Chel is a fanged, clawed, and dilapidated crone. However, in conjunction with the duplicity of Maya beings, Ix Chel also has a more benevolent form. She occasionally represents fertility and childbirth and, in these contexts, she is pictured as youthful and beautiful.",
      "locator": "Ix Chel",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam wujud lazimnya, Ix Chel adalah perempuan renta bertaring dan bercakar. Namun, selaras dengan sifat ganda makhluk Maya, ia juga punya wujud yang lebih ramah: kadang ia melambangkan kesuburan dan persalinan, dan dalam konteks itu digambarkan muda dan cantik.",
        "en": "In her typical form Ix Chel is a fanged, clawed, dilapidated crone, but she also has a more benevolent form in which she occasionally represents fertility and childbirth and is pictured as youthful and beautiful."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c19",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s4",
      "quote": "On the island of Cozumel shrines to the Virgin Mary and the goddess Ixchel are interchangeable and, often, one and the same.",
      "locator": "Legacy",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Pulau Cozumel, kuil-kuil bagi Perawan Maria dan dewi Ixchel dapat saling tertukar dan sering kali satu dan sama.",
        "en": "On the island of Cozumel, shrines to the Virgin Mary and the goddess Ixchel are interchangeable and often one and the same."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c20",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "In the 2006 movie Apocalypto, Ixchel is muttered by a Maya mother in a scene where she is forced to cross a river and her children are left behind.",
      "locator": "In popular culture",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam film Apocalypto (2006), nama Ixchel diucapkan lirih oleh seorang ibu Maya dalam adegan ketika ia dipaksa menyeberangi sungai dan anak-anaknya tertinggal.",
        "en": "In the 2006 movie Apocalypto, Ixchel is muttered by a Maya mother in a scene where she is forced to cross a river and her children are left behind."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ixchel-c21",
      "source_id": "ixchel-s1",
      "quote": "Ixchel is playable in Smite as a mage. She is the second Maya goddess to feature in Smite, after Awilix.",
      "locator": "In popular culture",

      "statement": {

        "en": "Ixchel is playable in Smite as a mage, the second Maya goddess to feature in Smite after Awilix."
      }

    "id": "Jackalope adalah hewan mitos Amerika Utara berupa jackrabbit bertanduk antelop, yang dipopulerkan lewat awetan taksidermi di Wyoming pada 1930-an.",
    "en": "The jackalope is a mythical animal of North America, a jackrabbit with antelope horns, popularized through taxidermy mounts in Wyoming in the 1930s.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Jackalope adalah hewan mitos dalam cerita rakyat Amerika Utara yang digambarkan sebagai jackrabbit bertanduk antelop; namanya gabungan jackrabbit dan antelope. Folklorwan memandangnya sebagai salah satu hewan cerita bualan yang disebut fearsome critters.",
      "en": "The jackalope is a mythical animal in North American folklore described as a jackrabbit with antelope horns, and its name blends jackrabbit and antelope. Folklorists regard it as one of the tall-tale animals called fearsome critters.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "jackalope-c01",
        "jackalope-c04",
        "jackalope-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pada 1930-an Douglas Herrick dan saudaranya, pemburu yang terampil taksidermi, mempopulerkan jackalope Amerika dengan menempelkan tanduk rusa pada bangkai jackrabbit dan menjualnya kepada sebuah hotel di Douglas, Wyoming. The New York Times mengaitkan asalnya dengan acara berburu tahun 1932, dan awetan pertama dijual seharga $10 kepada Roy Ball, yang memajangnya di La Bonte Hotel; kepalanya dicuri pada 1977.",
      "en": "In the 1930s Douglas Herrick and his brother, hunters skilled in taxidermy, made the American jackalope famous by fixing deer antlers to a jackrabbit carcass and selling the result to a hotel in Douglas, Wyoming. The New York Times traces its origin to a 1932 hunting outing, and the first mount was sold for $10 to Roy Ball, who displayed it in the La Bonte Hotel; its head was stolen in 1977.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "jackalope-c02",
        "jackalope-c11",
        "jackalope-c12"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Legenda dasarnya mungkin berkaitan dengan cerita serupa dalam budaya lain: sebuah karya Persia abad ke-13 menggambarkan kelinci bertanduk tunggal, wolpertinger muncul di Bavaria, dan legenda Huichol menyebut kelinci bertanduk. Pada akhir abad ke-18 ilmuwan umumnya menolak hare bertanduk sebagai spesies biologis, dan para peneliti menduga sebagian cerita itu terinspirasi oleh kelinci yang terinfeksi virus papiloma Shope, yang menumbuhkan benjolan mirip tanduk.",
      "en": "The underlying legend may be related to similar stories in other cultures: a Persian work of the 13th century shows a one-horned rabbit, the wolpertinger belongs to Bavaria, and Huichol legends speak of a horned rabbit.       "en": "Tall tales say jackalopes are so dangerous that hunters are told to strap stovepipes to their legs to avoid being gored, that they can imitate human voices, that they breed only during lightning, and that they can be lured with whiskey.   "sources": [
    {
      "id": "jackalope-s1",

    {
      "id": "jackalope-s2",
      "author": "Rhiannon Jakopak",
      "published": null,
      "language": "en",
      "accessed": "2026-10-06",
      "type": "news",
      "title": "Bar Room Banter: Jackalopes Are Real",
      "url": "https://www.themeateater.com/conservation/wildlife-management/bar-room-banter-jackalopes-are-real",
      "publisher": "MeatEater"

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c01",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "The word jackalope is a portmanteau of jackrabbit and antelope.",
      "locator": "Name",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata jackalope adalah gabungan (portmanteau) dari jackrabbit dan antelope.",
        "en": "The word jackalope is a portmanteau of jackrabbit and antelope."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c02",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "In the 1930s, Douglas Herrick and his brother, hunters with taxidermy skills, popularized the American jackalope by grafting deer antlers onto a jackrabbit carcass and selling the combination to a local hotel in Douglas, Wyoming.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 1930-an, Douglas Herrick dan saudaranya, pemburu yang terampil membuat awetan taksidermi, mempopulerkan jackalope Amerika dengan menempelkan tanduk rusa pada bangkai jackrabbit dan menjual hasilnya kepada sebuah hotel setempat di Douglas, Wyoming.",
        "en": "In the 1930s Douglas Herrick and his brother, hunters with taxidermy skills, popularized the American jackalope by grafting deer antlers onto a jackrabbit carcass and selling it to a local hotel in Douglas, Wyoming."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c03",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "Researchers suggest that at least some of the tales of horned hares were inspired by sightings of rabbits infected with the Shope papilloma virus.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para peneliti menduga sebagian cerita tentang hare bertanduk terinspirasi oleh penampakan kelinci yang terinfeksi virus papiloma Shope.",
        "en": "Researchers suggest that at least some of the tales of horned hares were inspired by sightings of rabbits infected with the Shope papilloma virus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c04",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "Folklorists see the jackalope as one of a group of tall tale animals, known as fearsome critters, common to North American culture since the turn of the twentieth century. These fabulous beasts appear in tall tales and lend themselves to comic hoaxes by entrepreneurs who seek attention for their own personal or the region's fortune.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para folklorwan memandang jackalope sebagai salah satu kelompok hewan cerita bualan, yang dikenal sebagai fearsome critters, yang lazim dalam budaya Amerika Utara sejak pergantian abad ke-20. Binatang-binatang ajaib ini muncul dalam cerita bualan dan cocok untuk tipuan jenaka oleh pengusaha yang mencari perhatian bagi keuntungan pribadi atau daerahnya.",
        "en": "Folklorists see the jackalope as one of a group of tall-tale animals, known as fearsome critters, common to North American culture since the turn of the twentieth century; these fabulous beasts appear in tall tales and suit comic hoaxes by entrepreneurs seeking attention for their own or the region's fortune."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c05",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "The jackalope is a mythical animal of North American folklore described as a jackrabbit with antelope horns.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jackalope adalah hewan mitos dalam cerita rakyat Amerika Utara yang digambarkan sebagai jackrabbit bertanduk antelop.",
        "en": "The jackalope is a mythical animal of North American folklore described as a jackrabbit with antelope horns."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c06",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "Stuffed and mounted, jackalopes are found in many bars and other places in the United States; stores catering to tourists sell jackalope postcards and other paraphernalia",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bentuk awetan, jackalope ditemukan di banyak bar dan tempat lain di Amerika Serikat; toko untuk wisatawan menjual kartu pos jackalope dan pernak-pernik lain.",
        "en": "Stuffed and mounted, jackalopes are found in many bars and other places in the United States, and stores catering to tourists sell jackalope postcards and other paraphernalia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c07",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "In 2005, the legislature of Wyoming considered a bill to make the jackalope the state's official mythological creature. It passed the House by a 45–12 margin, but the session ended before the Senate could take up the bill, which died.",
      "locator": "Official recognition",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2005, legislatur Wyoming mempertimbangkan rancangan undang-undang untuk menjadikan jackalope makhluk mitologis resmi negara bagian. RUU itu lolos di DPR dengan selisih 45–12, tetapi sidang berakhir sebelum Senat membahasnya sehingga RUU itu gugur.",
        "en": "In 2005 the Wyoming legislature considered a bill to make the jackalope the state's official mythological creature; it passed the House 45–12 but the session ended before the Senate could take it up, and it died."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c08",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "A 13th-century Persian work depicts a rabbit with a single horn, like a unicorn. In Europe, the horned rabbit appeared in Medieval and Renaissance folklore in Bavaria (the wolpertinger) and elsewhere.",
      "locator": "Origins",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebuah karya Persia abad ke-13 menggambarkan kelinci bertanduk tunggal seperti unicorn. Di Eropa, kelinci bertanduk muncul dalam cerita rakyat Abad Pertengahan dan Renaisans di Bavaria (wolpertinger) dan tempat lain.",
        "en": "A 13th-century Persian work depicts a rabbit with a single horn, like a unicorn, and in Europe the horned rabbit appeared in Medieval and Renaissance folklore in Bavaria (the wolpertinger) and elsewhere."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c09",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "These early scientific texts described and illustrated the hybrids as though they were real creatures, but by the end of the 18th century scientists generally rejected the idea of horned hares as a biological species.",
      "locator": "Origins",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Teks-teks ilmiah awal itu menguraikan dan menggambarkan hibrida tersebut seolah makhluk nyata, tetapi pada akhir abad ke-18 para ilmuwan umumnya menolak gagasan hare bertanduk sebagai spesies biologis.",
        "en": "These early scientific texts described and illustrated the hybrids as though they were real creatures, but by the end of the 18th century scientists generally rejected the idea of horned hares as a biological species."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c10",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "In Central America, mythological references to a horned rabbit creature can be found in Huichol legends. The Huichol oral tradition has passed down tales of a horned rabbit and of the deer getting horns from the rabbit.",
      "locator": "Origins",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Amerika Tengah, rujukan mitologis pada makhluk kelinci bertanduk terdapat dalam legenda Huichol. Tradisi lisan Huichol mewariskan kisah tentang kelinci bertanduk dan tentang rusa yang mendapat tanduk dari kelinci itu.",
        "en": "In Central America mythological references to a horned rabbit creature are found in Huichol legends, whose oral tradition has passed down tales of a horned rabbit and of the deer getting horns from the rabbit."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c11",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "The New York Times attributes the American jackalope's origin to a 1932 hunting outing involving Douglas Herrick (1920–2003) of Douglas, Wyoming.",
      "locator": "Douglas variant",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "The New York Times mengaitkan asal jackalope Amerika dengan sebuah acara berburu tahun 1932 yang melibatkan Douglas Herrick (1920–2003) dari Douglas, Wyoming.",
        "en": "The New York Times attributes the American jackalope's origin to a 1932 hunting outing involving Douglas Herrick (1920–2003) of Douglas, Wyoming."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c12",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "The first jackalope the brothers put together was sold for $10 to Roy Ball, who displayed it in Douglas' La Bonte Hotel. The mounted head was stolen in 1977.",
      "locator": "Douglas variant",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jackalope pertama yang dirakit kedua bersaudara itu dijual seharga $10 kepada Roy Ball, yang memajangnya di La Bonte Hotel di Douglas. Kepala awetan itu dicuri pada 1977.",
        "en": "The first jackalope the brothers put together was sold for $10 to Roy Ball, who displayed it in Douglas' La Bonte Hotel; the mounted head was stolen in 1977."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c13",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "The jackalope became a popular local attraction in Douglas, where the Chamber of Commerce issues Jackalope Hunting Licenses to tourists. The tags are good for hunting during official jackalope season, which occurs for only one day: June 31 (a nonexistent date as June has 30 days), from midnight to 2 a.m.",
      "locator": "Douglas variant",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jackalope menjadi atraksi lokal populer di Douglas, tempat Kamar Dagang menerbitkan Lisensi Berburu Jackalope bagi wisatawan. Tanda itu berlaku untuk berburu pada musim jackalope resmi yang hanya satu hari: 31 Juni (tanggal yang tidak ada karena Juni hanya 30 hari), dari tengah malam hingga pukul 2 pagi.",
        "en": "The jackalope became a popular local attraction in Douglas, where the Chamber of Commerce issues Jackalope Hunting Licenses to tourists, good for the one-day official jackalope season on June 31 (a nonexistent date), from midnight to 2 a.m."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c14",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "Jackalopes are said to be so dangerous that hunters are advised to wear stovepipes on their legs to keep from being gored.",
      "locator": "Tall tales",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jackalope dikatakan begitu berbahaya sehingga pemburu dianjurkan memakai pipa cerobong di kaki agar tidak ditanduk.",
        "en": "Jackalopes are said to be so dangerous that hunters are advised to wear stovepipes on their legs to keep from being gored."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c15",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "The jackalope can imitate the human voice, according to legend. During the days of the Old West, when cowboys gathered by the campfires singing at night, jackalopes could be heard mimicking their voices or singing along, usually as a tenor.",
      "locator": "Tall tales",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, jackalope dapat menirukan suara manusia. Pada masa Wild West, ketika koboi berkumpul di api unggun dan bernyanyi di malam hari, jackalope terdengar menirukan suara mereka atau ikut bernyanyi, biasanya sebagai tenor.",
        "en": "According to legend the jackalope can imitate the human voice; in the Old West, when cowboys sang around campfires at night, jackalopes could be heard mimicking them or singing along, usually as a tenor."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c16",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "One of the ways to catch a jackalope is to entice it with whiskey, the jackalope's beverage of choice.",
      "locator": "Tall tales",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salah satu cara menangkap jackalope adalah memikatnya dengan wiski, minuman favoritnya.",
        "en": "One of the ways to catch a jackalope is to entice it with whiskey, the jackalope's beverage of choice."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c17",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "It is said that jackalopes, the rare Lepus antilocapra, breed only during lightning flashes and that their antlers make the act difficult despite the hare's reputation for fertility.",
      "locator": "Tall tales",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dikatakan bahwa jackalope, Lepus antilocapra yang langka, hanya berkembang biak saat kilat menyambar dan bahwa tanduknya mempersulit hal itu meskipun hare terkenal subur.",
        "en": "It is said that jackalopes, the rare Lepus antilocapra, breed only during lightning flashes and that their antlers make the act difficult despite the hare's reputation for fertility."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c18",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "According to Dorson, in Mythical Creatures of the North Country (1969), historian Walker D. Wyman acknowledged the existence of what he called the Alkali Area Jackalope of the western United States. However, he expressed doubt that it predated the Jack-pine Jackalope of Minnesota and Wisconsin",
      "locator": "Douglas variant",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Dorson, dalam Mythical Creatures of the North Country (1969), sejarawan Walker D. Wyman mengakui adanya apa yang ia sebut Jackalope Area Alkali di Amerika Serikat bagian barat, tetapi meragukan bahwa ia lebih tua daripada Jackalope Jack-pine di Minnesota dan Wisconsin.",
        "en": "According to Dorson, in Mythical Creatures of the North Country (1969), historian Walker D. Wyman acknowledged the Alkali Area Jackalope of the western United States but doubted it predated the Jack-pine Jackalope of Minnesota and Wisconsin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c19",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "The underlying legend of the jackalope, upon which the Wyoming taxidermists were building, may be related to similar stories in other cultures and other historical times.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda dasar jackalope, yang dikembangkan para taksidermis Wyoming, mungkin berkaitan dengan cerita serupa dalam budaya dan masa sejarah lain.",
        "en": "The underlying legend of the jackalope, which the Wyoming taxidermists were building on, may be related to similar stories in other cultures and other historical times."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c20",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "Among them is Juan and the Jackalope: A Children's Book in Verse by Rudolfo Anaya.",
      "locator": "In popular culture",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salah satunya adalah Juan and the Jackalope: A Children's Book in Verse karya Rudolfo Anaya.",
        "en": "Among the books featuring the jackalope is Juan and the Jackalope: A Children's Book in Verse by Rudolfo Anaya."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c21",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s1",
      "quote": "A short story, \"Jackalope Wives\" by Ursula Vernon, has been nominated for a 2014 Nebula Award.",
      "locator": "In popular culture",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cerpen \"Jackalope Wives\" karya Ursula Vernon dinominasikan untuk Nebula Award 2014.",
        "en": "A short story, \"Jackalope Wives\" by Ursula Vernon, has been nominated for a 2014 Nebula Award."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c22",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s2",
      "quote": "Ralph and Douglas Herrick, taxidermists in Douglas, Wyoming, admitted to conjuring up the American jackalope that we know today. After returning from a hunting trip, the Herrick Brothers serendipitously noticed a dead jackrabbit lying next to a pair of antlers on the floor of their taxidermy shop.",
      "locator": "Herrick brothers",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ralph dan Douglas Herrick, taksidermis di Douglas, Wyoming, mengaku telah menciptakan jackalope Amerika yang kita kenal sekarang. Sepulang berburu, kedua Herrick itu kebetulan melihat jackrabbit mati tergeletak di samping sepasang tanduk di lantai bengkel taksidermi mereka.",
        "en": "Ralph and Douglas Herrick, taxidermists in Douglas, Wyoming, admitted to conjuring up the American jackalope we know today; after a hunting trip they happened to notice a dead jackrabbit lying next to a pair of antlers on the floor of their taxidermy shop."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c23",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s2",
      "quote": "Over time, Douglas, Wyoming, became known as the Jackalope Capital of the World, and eventually put up an 8-foot jackalope statue in the city center.",
      "locator": "Herrick brothers",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seiring waktu, Douglas, Wyoming, dikenal sebagai Ibu Kota Jackalope Dunia dan akhirnya memasang patung jackalope setinggi 8 kaki di pusat kota.",
        "en": "Over time Douglas, Wyoming, became known as the Jackalope Capital of the World and eventually put up an 8-foot jackalope statue in the city center."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jackalope-c24",
      "source_id": "jackalope-s2",
      "quote": "We see jackalopes in art, gas stations, and occasionally even featured in bills brought up by state legislatures.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jackalope terlihat dalam seni, di pom bensin, dan kadang bahkan menjadi bahan rancangan undang-undang yang diajukan legislatur negara bagian.",
        "en": "Jackalopes are seen in art, gas stations, and occasionally even in bills brought up by state legislatures."
      }
    }
  ],

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "White Lady adalah hantu perempuan berbaju putih yang dikaitkan dengan legenda tragedi setempat dan ditemukan dalam cerita rakyat banyak negara.",
    "en": "A White Lady is a female ghost in white linked to local tragedy legends and found in the folklore of many countries.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "white-lady-c01",
      "white-lady-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "White Lady (atau perempuan berbaju putih) adalah jenis hantu perempuan, biasanya berpakaian putih, yang dilaporkan terlihat di daerah pedesaan dan dikaitkan dengan legenda tragedi setempat. Legendanya ditemukan di banyak negara, dan banyak di antaranya bertema kematian dini, duka, pengkhianatan, atau cinta tak terpenuhi. Dalam banyak kisah ia dipercaya roh perempuan yang meninggal karena pembunuhan, bunuh diri, kecelakaan, atau ditinggalkan.",
      "en": "The White Lady, also called the woman in white, is a kind of female ghost usually clad in white, reportedly sighted in rural places and tied to local tragic legends. Her legends are found in many countries, and many share themes of untimely death, grief, betrayal, or unfulfilled love. In many accounts she is believed to be the spirit of a woman who died by murder, suicide, accident, or abandonment.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "white-lady-c01",
        "white-lady-c02",
        "white-lady-c03",
        "white-lady-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sebagian legenda menggambarkannya sebagai penampakan bisu atau pertanda malapetaka, sementara yang lain menceritakan ia meminta bantuan pengelana sebelum lenyap. Dalam legenda populer abad pertengahan ia muncul di rumah tempat anggota keluarga akan segera meninggal, dan satu ensiklopedia lama menyebutnya hantu leluhur perempuan. Di Berliner Schloss, hantu white lady yang dianggap pertanda kematian dilaporkan terlihat pada 1598 sebelum kematian John George dari Brandenburg.",
      "en": "Some legends portray her as a silent apparition or omen of misfortune, while others tell of her asking travelers for help before vanishing. In popular medieval legend she appears in a house where a family member is soon to die, and an old encyclopedia calls her the ghost of a deceased ancestress. At the Berliner Schloss, a white lady ghost regarded as a harbinger of death was reported in 1598 before the death of John George of Brandenburg.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "white-lady-c03",
        "white-lady-c06",
        "white-lady-c19",
        "white-lady-c12"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Tradisi setempat berbeda-beda: Dama Branca dalam bahasa Portugis, Dames blanches dalam bahasa Prancis, Weiße Frauen di Jerman, Witte Wieven di Belanda yang dapat bersifat baik dan jahat, kaperosa di Filipina, serta white lady Kastil Haapsalu di Estonia, Kastil Rožmberk di Republik Ceko, Kastil Rouelbeau di kanton Jenewa, dan Charles Fort di Irlandia. Dalam mitologi Hungaria ia hantu gadis atau perempuan muda yang meninggal secara kekerasan.",
      "en": "Local traditions differ: Dama Branca in Portuguese, Dames blanches in French, Weiße Frauen in Germany, Witte Wieven in the Netherlands who can be both benevolent and malevolent, kaperosa in the Philippines, and the white ladies of Haapsalu Castle in Estonia, Rožmberk Castle in the Czech Republic, Rouelbeau Castle in the canton of Geneva, and Charles Fort in Ireland. In Hungarian mythology she is the ghost of a girl or young woman who died violently.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "white-lady-c04",
        "white-lady-c07",
        "white-lady-c09",
        "white-lady-c10",
        "white-lady-c11",
        "white-lady-c13",
        "white-lady-c14",
        "white-lady-c16",
        "white-lady-c17",
        "white-lady-c18"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Banyak tradisi White Lady memiliki tema berulang kematian dini, duka, pengkhianatan, atau cinta yang tak terpenuhi, dan legendanya terikat pada tempat-tempat setempat. Dalam sebuah kamus cerita rakyat Brasil, hantu ini dikaitkan dengan pembunuhan \"demi kehormatan\" terhadap perempuan muda.",
    "en": "Across many White Lady traditions the same motifs return: a death before one's time, mourning, being betrayed, or love left unfulfilled, and each legend is anchored to a local place. A Brazilian folklore dictionary links the ghost to \"honor\" killings of young women.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "white-lady-c02",
      "white-lady-c05",
      "white-lady-c08"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": null,

    "famous_for": {

      ]

  },
  "did_you_know": {
    "id": "Di Filipina, banyak sumber mengatakan seorang wartawan merekayasa legenda White Lady dari Balete Drive pada 1950-an sebagai gabungan beberapa cerita dari kawasan itu.",
    "en": "In the Philippines, many sources say a reporter manufactured the Balete Drive White Lady legend in the 1950s as a combination of several stories from the area.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "white-lady-c15"
    ]
  },

  "stories": [
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "White lady Kastil Haapsalu",
        "en": "The white lady of Haapsalu Castle"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Perempuan yang jatuh cinta pada seorang kanon dan ditembok di dinding kapel.",
        "en": "A woman loved by a canon and immured in the chapel wall."
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Seorang kanon jatuh cinta padanya sehingga ia bersembunyi di kastil dengan menyamar sebagai penyanyi koor laki-laki. Ia ketahuan ketika Uskup Ösel-Wiek berkunjung, lalu ditembok di dinding kapel karena kejahatannya.",
        "en": "Because a canon had fallen for her, she concealed herself in the castle dressed as a choir boy; the Bishop of Ösel-Wiek found her out on a visit, and she was walled into the chapel as punishment."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "white-lady-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Dame blanche Air Terjun Montmorency",
        "en": "The Dame blanche of Montmorency Falls"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Roh perempuan muda yang menghantui sekitar air terjun.",
        "en": "The spirit of a young woman haunting the surroundings of the falls."
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Menurut satu legenda, sekitar Air Terjun Montmorency dihantui roh perempuan muda Prancis-Kanada yang tunangannya tewas dalam Pertempuran Beauport.",
        "en": "One legend holds that the area around Montmorency Falls is haunted by a young French-Canadian woman's spirit; her fiancé had died in the Battle of Beauport."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "white-lady-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "White lady Berliner Schloss",
        "en": "The white lady of the Berliner Schloss"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Pertanda kematian dan malapetaka.",
        "en": "A harbinger of death and misfortune."
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Hantu white lady diklaim terlihat di Berliner Schloss pada 1598 tak lama sebelum kematian John George dari Brandenburg, dan lagi pada 1618, 1625, hingga 1940.",
        "en": "Sightings of a white lady ghost were claimed at the Berliner Schloss in 1598, not long before John George of Brandenburg died, and again in 1618, 1625, and later up to 1940."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "white-lady-c12"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "White Lady dari Balete Drive",
        "en": "The White Lady of Balete Drive"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Hantu perempuan berambut panjang bergaun putih.",
        "en": "A long-haired ghost in a white dress."
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, ia hantu perempuan yang tewas dalam kecelakaan mobil di Balete Drive, Quezon City; banyak sumber mengatakan seorang wartawan merekayasa legenda ini pada 1950-an.",
        "en": "According to legend she is the ghost of a woman who died in a car accident on Balete Drive, Quezon City; many sources say a reporter manufactured the legend in the 1950s."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "white-lady-c14",
        "white-lady-c15"
      ]
    }

  "learning_questions": [
    {
      "id": "Mengapa begitu banyak budaya memiliki hantu perempuan berbaju putih yang terkait kehilangan atau pengkhianatan?",

      "source_id": "white-lady-s1",
      "quote": "A White Lady (or woman in white) is a type of female ghost, typically dressed in a white dress or similar garment, reportedly seen in rural areas and associated with local legends of tragedy.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "White Lady (atau perempuan berbaju putih) adalah jenis hantu perempuan, biasanya berbalut gaun putih atau pakaian serupa, yang dilaporkan terlihat di daerah pedesaan dan dikaitkan dengan legenda tragedi setempat.",
        "en": "A White Lady (or woman in white) is a type of female ghost, typically dressed in a white dress or similar garment, reportedly seen in rural areas and associated with local legends of tragedy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "white-lady-c02",
      "source_id": "white-lady-s1",
      "quote": "Although the details differ, many White Lady traditions share recurring themes of untimely death, grief, betrayal, or unfulfilled love.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meskipun rinciannya berbeda, banyak tradisi White Lady memiliki tema berulang: kematian dini, duka, pengkhianatan, atau cinta yang tak terpenuhi.",
        "en": "Although the details differ, many White Lady traditions share recurring themes of untimely death, grief, betrayal, or unfulfilled love."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "white-lady-c03",
      "source_id": "white-lady-s1",
      "quote": "In numerous accounts, the White Lady is believed to be the spirit of a woman who died through murder, suicide, accident, or abandonment. Some legends portray her as a silent apparition or an omen of misfortune, while others describe encounters in which she asks travelers for assistance before mysteriously disappearing.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam banyak kisah, White Lady dipercaya sebagai roh perempuan yang meninggal karena pembunuhan, bunuh diri, kecelakaan, atau ditinggalkan. Sebagian legenda menggambarkannya sebagai penampakan bisu atau pertanda malapetaka, sementara yang lain menceritakan perjumpaan ketika ia meminta bantuan pengelana sebelum lenyap secara misterius.",
        "en": "In numerous accounts the White Lady is believed to be the spirit of a woman who died through murder, suicide, accident, or abandonment; some legends portray her as a silent apparition or an omen of misfortune, others describe her asking travelers for help before mysteriously disappearing."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "white-lady-c04",
      "source_id": "white-lady-s1",
      "quote": "White Ladies are called Dames blanches in French. One legend claims that the surroundings of Montmorency Falls near Quebec City are haunted by a white lady. It is said to be the spirit of a young French-Canadian woman whose fiancé was killed while fighting against the British in the Battle of Beauport.",
      "locator": "Canada",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bahasa Prancis, White Lady disebut Dames blanches. Sebuah legenda menyebut kawasan sekitar Air Terjun Montmorency dekat Kota Quebec dihantui seorang white lady, yang dikatakan roh perempuan muda Prancis-Kanada yang tunangannya tewas dalam pertempuran melawan Inggris di Pertempuran Beauport.",
        "en": "White Ladies are called Dames blanches in French; one legend says the surroundings of Montmorency Falls near Quebec City are haunted by a white lady, said to be the spirit of a young French-Canadian woman whose fiancé was killed fighting the British in the Battle of Beauport."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "white-lady-c05",
      "source_id": "white-lady-s1",
      "quote": "White Lady legends are found in many countries around the world. Common to many of these legends is an accidental or impending death, murder, or suicide and the theme of loss, betrayed by a husband or fiancé, and unrequited love.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda White Lady ditemukan di banyak negara. Umum pada banyak legenda itu adalah kematian karena kecelakaan atau yang akan datang, pembunuhan, atau bunuh diri, serta tema kehilangan, dikhianati suami atau tunangan, dan cinta tak berbalas.",
        "en": "White Lady legends are found in many countries around the world, and common to many are an accidental or impending death, murder, or suicide, and themes of loss, betrayal by a husband or fiancé, and unrequited love."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "white-lady-c06",
      "source_id": "white-lady-s1",

      "statement": {

        "en": "In popular medieval legend a White Lady is fabled to appear by day as well as by night in a house in which a family member is soon to die, and they also appear within photos just before or after death."

      "quote": "Called Dama Branca or Mulher de Branco in Portuguese, the Brazilian Lady in White is said to be the ghost of a young woman who died of childbirth or violent causes.
